Machine: Node v24.16.0 (C:\Program Files\nodejs), git 2.54.0, wrangler 4.152.0.

Run conditions that matter (two environment problems found and fixed first):
1. A Goose-app portable Node 22.14.0 npx shim (C:\Users\User\AppData\Local\Goose\bin\npx.cmd,
   ahead of the real Node in PATH) hijacked `npx`, so vitest ran under Node 22 and every
   spawn of `node <script>.ts` failed with ERR_UNKNOWN_FILE_EXTENSION. Fix for this run:
   PATH prefixed with C:\Program Files\nodejs. Martin: consider moving the Goose shim behind
   the real Node in PATH (affects everything that calls npx on this machine).
2. On Windows each `npx wrangler` spawn costs ~3.3s (npm CLI startup ~1.8s + wrangler load ~1.5s;
   Linux ~1s total). Under full-suite load this pushed tests/node/operator.test.ts over the 5s
   default vitest timeout (the timed-out test passed in isolation at ~3.5s). Uncommitted fix,
   not a test change: scripts/operator-lib.ts runSql and tests/node/operator.test.ts beforeAll
   now invoke node_modules/wrangler/bin/wrangler.js with process.execPath directly
   (drops the shell too). operator.test.ts file time: 186s -> 76s; the borderline test 3.5s -> 1.5s.
   No assertion was weakened, removed or re-timed.
